    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

95.10% (194/204), FOL parser 204/204 全成功。Per-type: True 97.2% (+4.1pp), False 93.6% (+6.3pp), Unknown 78.3% (+31.9pp)。与 exp-021 (88.24%, 旧 traces) 对比：oracle 准确率从 88.24% 提升至 89.71%（+1.47pp），delta 从 **exp-074d-icg-mistral-folio-seed456** (negative): ICG × Mistral-7B × FOLIO-204 seed=456 第三组 seed replication。exp-074 (seed=42) Δ=-2.0pp, exp-074c (seed=123) 运行中。3-seed 数据确认 ICG 方法的 negative 结果跨 seed 稳定性，堵住 reviewer 单 seed 质疑。ICG 是三方法中 Δ 最接近零的，3-seed 数据最有论文价值。 ← 基于 exp-074-icg-mistral-folio\\n- **exp-077-kappa-generalization** (negative): High Fleiss kappa (low independence) is a general property of same-model aggregation, not specific to SC+SICA. LLM-Debate and Diverse-Prompt aggregation on Mistral-7B will also exhibit high kappa and low Effective K, confirming the independence bottleneck is fundamental to single-model ensembles regardless of aggregation method.\\n- **exp-077** (negative): All three LLM aggregation methods (Self-Consistency, LLM-Debate, Diverse-Prompt) suffer from the same independence bottleneck (Fleiss' κ > 0.4), because the shared model backbone forces correlated reasoning traces regardless of the aggregation strategy used.\\n\\n## 趋势\\n成功 28 / 负向 55 / 失败 19 / 共 114\\ndelta: 0.02 ↑（近 3 次 success）\\nn_samples: 200 →（近 -1.9 |\\n\\n(注: SC/Δ 使用 pre-tiebreaking 值，entropy grouping 不受 tiebreaking 影响。D064 修正后 deltas 会略有变化但 pattern 不变。)\\n\\n**三个核心发现：**\\n1. **Low-entropy: SICA≈SC 跨模型一致** (8/8 experiments Δ≈0) → traces 一致时 constraints 无新信息\\n2. **High-entropy 方向因 regime 而异**：Goldilocks +12.8pp vs Type A -8.2pp vs Type C -4.9pp\\n3. **Mechanism 解释**：\\n   - Goldilocks (SC~55%, κ~0.46): 真实不确定性 → constraints 打破平局正确方向\\n   - Type A (SC>75%, κ>0.65): 偶发错误 → constraints 放大 confirmation bias\\n   - Type C (SC<40%, κ~0.20): 系统性错误 → constraints 是噪声\\n\\n### 调整后核心转变\\n\\n论文以 \\\"entropy decomposition 揭示 regime-dependent mechanism\\\" 为核心分析贡献（不是 universal diagnostic）：\\n- **Entropy decomposition 作为分析工具**：分离 SICA 作用点（high-entropy）vs 不作用点（low-entropy）\\n- **Regime mechanism 量化**：同样是 high-entropy 问题，direction depends on SC+κ → 完美支持 dual bottleneck\\n- **Abandoned**: Selective SICA 作为 universal practical contribution（只在 Goldilocks zone 有效）\\n- **Upgraded**: 核心图 = high-entropy Δ vs SC scatter（8 points, 3 regimes 清晰分离）\\n- **Quality Ladder** (D067: L1 NULL CONFIRMED)\\n  - L0: Self-extraction (Mistral→Mistral): 59.31%, Δ=+3.43pp ns\\n  - L1a: Qwen3-14B→Mistral traces: 60.29%, Δ=+4.41pp (+0.98pp vs self = 2题/204 = noise)\\n  - L1b: Qwen2.5-14B→Mistral traces: 58.33%, Δ=+2.45pp (-0.98pp vs self = 2题/204 = noise)\\n  - L3: Oracle 100% disc, Δ=+14.22pp p<0.001\\n  - **结论**: L1 ≈ L0 << L3。Bottleneck 在 traces（同源输入），不在 extractor（换模型无效）\\n  - **Paper contribution**: \\\"bottleneck is fundamental to extraction, not just model capability\\\" — 强化 structural limitation 论点\\n- **CJT/Eff-K 保留**：解释 why high-entropy behavior differs across regimes\\n\\nD060 要素全部保留：\\n- Condorcet independence 框架 → 理论基础\\n- 9 种修复全部失败 → structural limitation 证据\\n- 2/3 seeds positive trend but **0/3 significant** (D066: p=0.092/0.070/1.0) → Goldilocks zone 作为 directional trend, not confirmed effect\\n- K/T sensitivity non-monotonic → SC confound insight\\n- 用户指示: 投 May 25 不变，冲 4.0\\n\\n## Core Claims (5 条) — D060 纯 Analysis Framing\\n\\n### Analysis Claims (已验证)\\n\\n1. **Independence Bottleneck**: Self-consistency 隐含 Condorcet Jury Theorem 的独立性假设，但同模型采样的 traces 高度相关。Worker 验证的 κ 值：\\n   - Qwen-14B FOLIO: κ=0.744, Eff-K=1.31 (SC=75.49%, Δ=+0.25pp n.s.)\\n   - Qwen-14B LogiQA: κ=0.821, Eff-K=1.20 (SC=78.5%, Δ=+0.50pp n.s.)\\n   - Mistral-7B FOLIO s42 (exp-033): κ=0.501, Eff-K=1.84 (SC=55.88%, Δ=+3.43pp **p=0.092 ns**) ← primary seed [D064+D066]\\n   - Mistral-7B FOLIO s123: κ=0.491, Eff-K=1.88 (SC=54.41%, Δ=+2.94pp **p=0.070 ns**)\\n   - Mistral-7B FOLIO s456: κ=0.507, Eff-K=1.82 (SC=55.88%, Δ=0.00pp p=1.0 ns)\\n   - Mistral-7B PW-600 (exp-046): κ=0.210, Eff-K=3.63 (Δ=-2.83pp p=0.010, SC=39%)\\n   规律：跨模型对比清晰（Qwen Eff-K≈1.2-1.3 → 0 gain; Mistral Eff-K≈1.85-2.22 → variable gain）。但 Mistral seeds 内部，Eff-K 与 delta 非单调（exp-033 Eff-K 最低 1.85 却 delta 最高 +3.43pp）→ Eff-K 是跨模型必要条件，seed 内 variance 仍主导。exp-046 Eff-K 最高(3.63) 但 SC 太低(39%) 导致 delta 为负 → 需同时满足 sufficient Eff-K AND sufficient SC。\\n\\n2. **Oracle Gap Localizes Extraction Bottleneck**: Gold FOL + MAX-SAT = +14.22pp (p=2.0e-7)，自提取仅 +3.43pp (best seed, D064 corrected) = 24% utilization。聚合机制本身有效，瓶颈在自提取质量。Two-layer analysis:\\n   - **Constraint taxonomy**: 43% trivially_satisfied（被所有候选答案满足的约束，不排除任何答案）\\n   - **Discriminative filtering**: 仅 ~8% 约束在 MAX-SAT scoring 中完全 non-discriminative（移除后 0 gains）\\n   - **关键区分**: 43% 和 8% 度量不同。trivially_satisfied 约束在 MAX-SAT 中仍可有不同满足程度（partial vs full），所以不全是 non-discriminative。但即使 constraints 是 discriminative 的，它们的\\\"区分方向\\\"与模型自身偏差一致（confirmation bias）。both_wrong 组 score_range=29.4。九种修复全部失败。\\n\\n3. **Regime-Dependent Capability Trap**: SICA 有效性取决于提取模型的基线准确率，存在双边界：\\n   - Type A (SC ≥ ~60%): 确认偏差，constraints 镜像模型自身答案。**exp-034 vs exp-039 同配置 delta 翻转**: exp-034 Δ=+0.51pp (n=589), exp-039 Δ=-2.00pp (n=600)。SC baseline 方差 (3.38pp) >> SICA 效应 → Type A delta 是 trace sampling noise。SICA 比 SC 更稳定 (跨运行方差 0.87pp vs 3.38pp，仅 2 点，需更多 seed 验证)\\n   - Type C (SC ≤ ~40%): traces 主要错误，constraints 是噪声 (exp-046: Δ=-2.83pp, p=0.0095)\\n   - Goldilocks zone (~50-56% SC): 部分独立性 + 部分正确性 (exp-033: Δ=+3.43pp, p=0.092 ns; mean of 3 seeds: +2.12pp)\\n   - Type B: 领域不可形式化 (LogiQA: contradiction rate 0.8%)\\n\\n4. **C0+SC Two-Dimensional Diagnostic**: C0 (multi-candidate rate) + SC accuracy 形成零成本部署诊断。C0<5% → degenerate; high C0 + high SC → trap; high C0 + low SC → potential gains。\\n\\n### Remediation Evidence (D060: 全部失败 — 强化 analysis narrative)\\n\\n5. **Nine Remediation Attempts All Failed**: 原 6 种 + 3 种靶向修复全部无效，证明 independence bottleneck 是结构性的：\\n   - D059 三路 method improvement 全部失败：\\n     - Discriminative Filtering: 119/204 处理后 0 gains 1 loss，~92% 约束本身就是 discriminative，过滤是 near-no-op\\n     - T=1.0 (exp-054): delta 从 +3.43pp 崩塌到 0.00pp（D064 corrected），温度敏感性极端\\n     - Multi-Temp (exp-055): ✅ 完成，delta=+0.00pp (b=5,c=5 完美对称), SC=57.84%. 混合 T=0.3/0.7/1.2 各 4 traces 未改善 SICA。附带发现：multi-temp 提升 SC baseline (+3.4pp vs 单温度 54.41%)，说明混合温度对 majority voting 有帮助，但 SICA constraint extraction 无法利用这种 diversity → bottleneck 在 extraction 而非 trace diversity\\n   - 原 6 种：contrastive prompting, cross-model extraction, generational scaling, solver-guided refinement, consistency filtering, cross-trace UNSAT\\n   - 9 种修复涵盖 prompting / architecture / capability / verification / diversity / filtering 全维度，全部失败 → trap is fundamental\\n\\n## D070/D074 Update: Method-Level Interventions Also Fail — \\\"Fundamental Limitation\\\" Confirmed\\n\\n### Three Method Lines × Two Datasets = All Negative (D074)\\n\\nD070 从纯 analysis 升级为 analysis + method paper，但三条方法线全面失败（D074 确认），反而强化了原始 analysis 结论。\\n\\n**方法×数据集完整结果**：\\n| Method | FOLIO-204 | LogiQA-200 | Failure Mode |\\n|--------|-----------|------------|--------------|\\n| ICG (Independent Constraint Gen.) | Δ=-2.0pp (3-seed: -2.0/+1.5/-1.0, mean=-0.5pp±1.8pp, 噪声) | Δ=-0.5pp | Neutral — 独立约束质量≈trace-sourced约束 |\\n| Contrastive (Pro/Con Scoring) | Δ≈-16pp | Δ≈-7pp (interim) | Harmful — 26% degenerate scoring, pro/con 不平衡 |\\n| Debate (Proposer/Critic) | Δ=-18.6pp | Δ≈-28pp (interim) | Catastrophic — 7B 模型角色扮演严重退化推理 |\\n\\n**三种失败模式的分析价值**（论文 \\\"Why Methods Fail\\\" section）：\\n1. **ICG (neutral)**: 独立于 traces 生成的约束不比 trace-sourced 约束更好。说明 bottleneck 不仅是 \\\"约束来自 traces\\\"，而是模型的推理偏差从问题理解阶段就存在——独立约束和 trace-sourced 约束继承相同的 model bias。\\n2. **Contrastive (harmful)**: 强制 pro/con 评估引入 degenerate scoring（26% 问题无法有效区分）。7B 模型无法可靠生成平衡的对立约束——pro 约束系统性多于 con（94 pro vs 65 con），confirmation bias 从约束提取转移到了约束生成。\\n3. **Debate (catastrophic)**: Proposer/Critic/Defense 多角色链在 7B 模型上导致答案质量严重退化。辩论不仅未增加多样性，反而引入了角色扮演的额外认知负担。同模型辩论 = 同源性 + 角色扮演退化，双重损失。\\n\\n**Scale limitation caveat**: 所有方法测试在 7B 模型上。7B 模型生成 Z3 格式 FOL 约束的能力不足（ICG 多题 \\\"0/N constraints parsed\\\"）。14B+ 模型可能有不同结果——这既是 limitation 也是 future work 入口。\\n\\n### κ Generalization — Independence-Accuracy Trade-off (D075, CAPSTONE FINDING)\\n\\nexp-077 测量三种聚合方式在 Mistral-7B FOLIO-204 上的 κ 和 Eff-K：\\n| 聚合方式 | Fleiss' κ | Eff-K | 名义 K |\\n|----------|----------|-------|--------|\\n| Self-Consistency | 0.4384 | 2.02 | 10 |\\n| Diverse-Prompt | 0.4030 | 2.21 | 12 |\\n| LLM-Debate | 0.1549 | 4.44 | 12 |\\n\\n结合 exp-076 Debate Δ=-18.6pp 的准确率数据，揭示**同模型框架下的 fundamental independence-accuracy trade-off**：\\n- **SC/Diverse-Prompt**: κ~0.4，traces 高度相关但准确率正常。换 prompt 策略不改善独立性（Δκ=0.04）。\\n- **Debate**: κ=0.15，成功打破 trace 相关性（Eff-K 从 2.0 跃升到 4.4），但准确率崩溃 -18.6pp。低 κ 不代表\\\"更独立的推理\\\"——是 disagreement amplification（系统性噪声）。\\n- **Trade-off 本质**: 同一模型的推理能力是固定的。要么输出一致（高 κ, 正确但不独立），要么输出分歧（低 κ, 独立但不正确）。Condorcet Jury Theorem 要求 both independent AND accurate，同模型框架下无法同时满足。\\n\\n**论文叙事升级**: 从 \\\"methods fail\\\" → \\\"independence-accuracy trade-off is fundamental to same-model reasoning\\\"。突破瓶颈需要跨模型方案（不同模型天然更独立），Oracle 实验（gold FOL 来自外部知识源）已间接验证了这一点。\\n\\n### New Claim 6 (D070/D074)\\n\\n6. **Method-Level Interventions Cannot Break the Bottleneck**: 三种针对 independence bottleneck 不同环节的方法（ICG→循环依赖, Contrastive→confirmation bias, Debate→trace 同质性）在 2 个数据集上全部失败。加上之前 9 种 prompt-level 修复，共 12 种修复尝试全败。这证明 independence bottleneck 不是单一环节的问题，而是 same-model self-extraction 的根本性限制。\\n\\n### Updated Headline Result (D074)\\n\\n\\\"Across 6+ models and 4 domains, symbolic consensus aggregation shows no statistically significant positive effect. Three method-level interventions designed to break specific aspects of the independence bottleneck (independent constraint generation, contrastive scoring, debate-augmented extraction) all fail across two datasets, with two methods actively degrading performance by 16-28pp. Combined with 9 prompt-level remediation failures, we provide the most comprehensive evidence to date that the independence bottleneck is fundamental to same-model reasoning aggregation.\\\"\\n\\n### Updated Story Spine (D074)\\n\\n- **Problem**: [unchanged]\\n- **Theoretical lens**: [unchanged]\\n- **Systematic evaluation**: [unchanged]\\n- **Regime characterization**: [unchanged]\\n- **Remediation failures strengthen thesis**: 9 prompt-level + 3 method-level = 12 interventions across diversity/architecture/independence/verification dimensions, all fail → bottleneck is fundamental\\n- **NEW — Method failure analysis**: Three methods targeting different bottleneck aspects show three distinct failure modes (neutral/harmful/catastrophic), revealing that the bottleneck operates at multiple levels simultaneously\\n- **Headline**: Independence bottleneck is structural to same-model self-extraction, not addressable by prompt engineering, architectural modifications, or targeted method-level interventions at the 7B scale\\n\\n## Core Insight (D060 Updated — Dual Bottleneck)\\n\\n**Dual Requirement Framework**: SICA 效应需要同时满足两个条件，缺一不可：\\n1. **Sufficient Independence** (Eff-K > threshold): traces 需要提供足够多的独立视角\\n2. **Sufficient Quality** (SC > ~50%): traces 需要有足够的正确率，否则约束是噪声\\n\\nCondorcet Jury Theorem 要求投票者独立且 p>0.5。同模型采样的 reasoning traces 严重违反独立性假设（Fleiss' κ = 0.21–0.82，Eff-K 仅 1.20–3.63），解释了为什么简单增加 K 或添加符号约束不能突破 SC 的天花板。\\n\\n实验数据完美支持 dual requirement（κ 使用统一脚本 compute_fleiss_kappa_unified.py，SC/Δpp 使用 registry 原始值）：\\n- **Qwen family**: κ=0.66-0.82, Eff-K≈1.20-1.46（独立性不足）→ all near-zero delta，无论 SC 多高\\n- **Mistral-7B FOLIO**: κ≈0.46, Eff-K≈2.0（中等独立性）+ SC≈55%（中等质量）→ +3.43pp best seed, mean +2.12pp (2/3 seeds positive, 1 null)\\n- **Mistral-7B PW-600**: κ=0.20, Eff-K=3.76（最高独立性）但 SC=39%（质量不足）→ -2.83pp\\n- 只有 Goldilocks zone（中等 Eff-K + 中等 SC）出现正向 delta\\n- **Dual bottleneck 直接验证 (exp-055)**：Multi-temp κ=0.427 Eff-K=2.11（比单温度 κ≈0.46 Eff-K≈2.0 更独立）但 delta=0.00pp → 提高 independence 不够，还需要 extraction quality\\n- **T sensitivity 孤立尖峰 (4/4 complete, D064 corrected)**：T=0.3(Δ=-1.47), T=0.5(Δ=-0.98), T=0.7(Δ=+3.43, κ=0.460, Eff-K=1.98), T=1.0(Δ=0.00, κ=0.394, Eff-K=2.25)。T=0.7 孤立尖峰更突出。T=0.7 SC=55.88%（最低）→ entropy 最高 → SICA 操作空间最大。温度效应与 SC 效应互为 confound，论文需讨论\\n- **Thinking mode κ=0.913, Eff-K=1.09 (exp-028b)**：近乎确定性。思维模式让 traces 更一致 → 独立性更差。\\\"更好的推理\\\"反而加剧 independence bottleneck。\\n\\n**κ 是 model-level 稳定属性**：统一处理后 Mistral 三 seed κ 收敛至 0.457-0.460（之前 0.41-0.50 差异是非标准 answer 处理的 artifact）。κ 跨 seed 几乎相同，但 delta 差异大（+3.43 vs +2.94 vs 0.00）→ seed-level constraint quality variance 主导，非 κ。\\n\\n完整 κ 表（⚠️ D062: κ 将用 raw answers 重算，当前 unified 值为临时值。Qwen 无变化，Mistral 条件将更新）：\\n| Exp | Model | κ(unified→raw pending) |\\n\\n... κ=0.491, Eff-K=1.88 (SC=54.41%, Δ=+2.94pp **p=0.070 ns**)\\\\n   - Mistral-7B FOLIO s456: κ=0.507, Eff-K=1.82 (SC=55.88%, Δ=0.00pp p=1.0 ns)\\\\n   - Mistral-7B PW-600 (exp-046): κ=0.210, Eff-K=3.63 (Δ=-2.83pp p=0.010, SC=39%)\\\\n   规律：跨模型对比清晰（Qwen Eff-K≈1.2-1.3 → 0 gain; Mistral Eff-K≈1.85-2.22 → variable gain）。但 Mistral seeds 内部，Eff-K 与 delta 非单调（exp-033 Eff-K 最低 1.85 却 delta 最高 +3.43pp）→ Eff-K 是跨模型必要条件，seed 内 variance 仍主导。exp-046 Eff-K 最高(3.63) 但 SC 太低(39%) 导致 delta 为负 → 需同时满足 sufficient Eff-K AND sufficient SC。\\\\n\\\\n2. **Oracle Gap Localizes Extraction Bottleneck**: Gold FOL + MAX-SAT = +14.22pp (p=2.0e-7)，自提取仅 +3.43pp (best seed, D064 corrected) = 24% utilization。聚合机制本身有效，瓶颈在自提取质量。Two-layer analysis:\\\\n   - **Constraint taxonomy**: 43% trivially_satisfied（被所有候选答案满足的约束，不排除任何答案）\\\\n   - **Discriminative filtering**: 仅 ~8% 约束在 MAX-SAT scoring 中完全 non-discriminative（移除后 0 gains）\\\\n   - **关键区分**: 43% 和 8% 度量不同。trivially_satisfied 约束在 MAX-SAT 中仍可有不同满足程度（partial vs full），所以不全是 non-discriminative。但即使 constraints 是 discriminative 的，它们的\\\\\\\"区分方向\\\\\\\"与模型自身偏差一致（confirmation bias）。both_wrong 组 score_range=29.4。九种修复全部失败。\\\\n\\\\n3. **Regime-Dependent Capability Trap**: SICA 有效性取决于提取模型的基线准确率，存在双边界：\\\\n   - Type A (SC ≥ ~60%): 确认偏差，constraints 镜像模型自身答案。**exp-034 vs exp-039 同配置 delta 翻转**: exp-034 Δ=+0.51pp (n=589), exp-039 Δ=-2.00pp (n=600)。SC baseline 方差 (3.38pp) >> SICA 效应 → Type A delta 是 trace sampling noise。SICA 比 SC 更稳定 (跨运行方差 0.87pp vs 3.38pp，仅 2 点，需更多 seed 验证)\\\\n   - Type C (SC ≤ ~40%): traces 主要错误，constraints 是噪声 (exp-046: Δ=-2.83pp, p=0.0095)\\\\n   - Goldilocks zone (~50-56% SC): 部分独立性 + 部分正确性 (exp-033: Δ=+3.43pp, p=0.092 ns; mean of 3 seeds: +2.12pp)\\\\n   - Type B: 领域不可形式化 (LogiQA: contradiction rate 0.8%)\\\\n\\\\n4. **C0+SC Two-Dimensional Diagnostic**: C0 (multi-candidate rate) + SC accuracy 形成零成本部署诊断。C0<5% → degenerate; high C0 + high SC → trap; high C0 + low SC → potential gains。\\\\n\\\\n### Remediation Evidence (D060: 全部失败 — 强化 analysis narrative)\\\\n\\\\n5. **Nine Remediation Attempts All Failed**: 原 6 种 + 3 种靶向修复全部无效，证明 independence bottleneck 是结构性的：\\\\n   - D059 三路 method improvement 全部失败：\\\\n     - Discriminative Filtering: 119/204 处理后 0 gains 1 loss，~92% 约束本身就是 discriminative，过滤是 near-no-op\\\\n     - T=1.0 (exp-054): delta 从 +3.43pp 崩塌到 0.00pp（D064 corrected），温度敏感性极端\\\\n     - Multi-Temp (exp-055): ✅ 完成，delta=+0.00pp (b=5,c=5 完美对称), SC=57.84%. 混合 T=0.3/0.7/1.2 各 4 traces 未改善 SICA。附带发现：multi-temp 提升 SC baseline (+3.4pp vs 单温度 54.41%)，说明混合温度对 majority voting 有帮助，但 SICA constraint extraction 无法利用这种 diversity → bottleneck 在 extraction 而非 trace diversity\\\\n   - 原 6 种：contrastive prompting, cross-model extraction, generational scaling, solver-guided refinement, consistency filtering, cross-trace UNSAT\\\\n   - 9 种修复涵盖 prompting / architecture / capability / verification / diversity / filtering 全维度，全部失败 → trap is fundamental\\\\n\\\\n## D070/D074 Update: Method-Level Interventions Also Fail — \\\\\\\"Fundamental Limitation\\\\\\\" Confirmed\\\\n\\\\n### Three Method Lines × Two Datasets = All Negative (D074)\\\\n\\\\nD070 从纯 analysis 升级为 analysis + method paper，但三条方法线全面失败（D074 确认），反而强化了原始 analysis 结论。\\\\n\\\\n**方法×数据集完整结果**：\\\\n| Method | FOLIO-204 | LogiQA-200 | Failure Mode |\\\\n|--------|-----------|------------|--------------|\\\\n| ICG (Independent Constraint Gen.) | Δ=-2.0pp (3-seed: -2.0/+1.5/-1.0, mean=-0.5pp±1.8pp, 噪声) | Δ=-0.5pp | Neutral — 独立约束质量≈trace-sourced约束 |\\\\n| Contrastive (Pro/Con Scoring) | Δ≈-16pp | Δ≈-7pp (interim) | Harmful — 26% degenerate scoring, pro/con 不平衡 |\\\\n| Debate (Proposer/Critic) | Δ=-18.6pp | Δ≈-28pp (interim) | Catastrophic — 7B 模型角色扮演严重退化推理 |\\\\n\\\\n**三种失败模式的分析价值**（论文 \\\\\\\"Why Methods Fail\\\\\\\" section）：\\\\n1. **ICG (neutral)**: 独立于 traces 生成的约束不比 trace-sourced 约束更好。说明 bottleneck 不仅是 \\\\\\\"约束来自 traces\\\\\\\"，而是模型的推理偏差从问题理解阶段就存在——独立约束和 trace-sourced 约束继承相同的 model bias。\\\\n2. **Contrastive (harmful)**: 强制 pro/con 评估引入 degenerate scoring（26% 问题无法有效区分）。7B 模型无法可靠生成平衡的对立约束——pro 约束系统性多于 con（94 pro vs 65 con），confirmation bias 从约束提取转移到了约束生成。\\\\n3. **Debate (catastrophic)**: Proposer/Critic/Defense 多角色链在 7B 模型上导致答案质量严重退化。辩论不仅未增加多样性，反而引入了角色扮演的额外认知负担。同模型辩论 = 同源性 + 角色扮演退化，双重损失。\\\\n\\\\n**Scale limitation caveat**: 所有方法测试在 7B 模型上。7B 模型生成 Z3 格式 FOL 约束的能力不足（ICG 多题 \\\\\\\"0/N constraints parsed\\\\\\\"）。14B+ 模型可能有不同结果——这既是 limitation 也是 future work 入口。\\\\n\\\\n### κ Generalization — Independence-Accuracy Trade-off (D075, CAPSTONE FINDING)\\\\n\\\\nexp-077 测量三种聚合方式在 Mistral-7B FOLIO-204 上的 κ 和 Eff-K：\\\\n| 聚合方式 | Fleiss' κ | Eff-K | 名义 K |\\\\n|----------|----------|-------|--------|\\\\n| Self-Consistency | 0.4384 | 2.02 | 10 |\\\\n| Diverse-Prompt | 0.4030 | 2.21 | 12 |\\\\n| LLM-Debate | 0.1549 | 4.44 | 12 |\\\\n\\\\n结合 exp-076 Debate Δ=-18.6pp 的准确率数据，揭示**同模型框架下的 fundamental independence-accuracy trade-off**：\\\\n- **SC/Diverse-Prompt**: κ~0.4，traces 高度相关但准确率正常。换 prompt 策略不改善独立性（Δκ=0.04）。\\\\n- **Debate**: κ=0.15，成功打破 trace 相关性（Eff-K 从 2.0 跃升到 4.4），但准确率崩溃 -18.6pp。低 κ 不代表\\\\\\\"更独立的推理\\\\\\\"——是 disagreement amplification（系统性噪声）。\\\\n- **Trade-off 本质**: 同一模型的推理能力是固定的。要么输出一致（高 κ, 正确但不独立），要么输出分歧（低 κ, 独立但不正确）。Condorcet Jury Theorem 要求 both independent AND accurate，同模型框架下无法同时满足。\\\\n\\\\n**论文叙事升级**: 从 \\\\\\\"methods fail\\\\\\\" → \\\\\\\"independence-accuracy trade-off is fundamental to same-model reasoning\\\\\\\"。突破瓶颈需要跨模型方案（不同模型天然更独立），Oracle 实验（gold FOL 来自外部知识源）已间接验证了这一点。\\\\n\\\\n### New Claim 6 (D070/D074)\\\\n\\\\n6. **Method-Level Interventions Cannot Break the Bottleneck**: 三种针对 independence bottleneck 不同环节的方法（ICG→循环依赖, Contrastive→confirmation bias, Debate→trace 同质性）在 2 个数据集上全部失败。加上之前 9 种 prompt-level 修复，共 12 种修复尝试全败。这证明 independence bottleneck 不是单一环节的问题，而是 same-model self-extraction 的根本性限制。\\\\n\\\\n### Updated Headline Result (D074)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\\\\"Across 6+ models and 4 domains, symbolic consensus aggregation shows no statistically significant positive effect. Three method-level interventions designed to break specific aspects of the independence bottleneck (independent constraint generation, contrastive scoring, debate-augmented extraction) all fail across two datasets, with two methods actively degrading performance by 16-28pp. Combined with 9 prompt-level remediation failures, we provide the most comprehensive evidence to date that the independence bottleneck is fundamental to same-model reasoning aggregation.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Updated Story Spine (D074)\\\\n\\\\n- **Problem**: [unchanged]\\\\n- **Theoretical lens**: [unchanged]\\\\n- **Systematic evaluation**: [unchanged]\\\\n- **Regime characterization**: [unchanged]\\\\n- **Remediation failures strengthen thesis**: 9 prompt-level + 3 method-level = 12 interventions across diversity/architecture/independence/verification dimensions, all fail → bottleneck is fundamental\\\\n- **NEW — Method failure analysis**: Three methods targeting different bottleneck aspects show three distinct failure modes (neutral/harmful/catastrophic), revealing that the bottleneck operates at multiple levels simultaneously\\\\n- **Headline**: Independence bottleneck is structural to same-model self-extraction, not addressable by prompt engineering, architectural modifications, or targeted method-level interventions at the 7B scale\\\\n\\\\n## Core Insight (D060 Updated — Dual Bottleneck)\\\\n\\\\n**Dual Requirement Framework**: SICA 效应需要同时满足两个条件，缺一不可：\\\\n1. **Sufficient Independence** (Eff-K > threshold): traces 需要提供足够多的独立视角\\\\n2. **Sufficient Quality** (SC > ~50%): traces 需要有足够的正确率，否则约束是噪声\\\\n\\\\nCondorcet Jury Theorem 要求投票者独立且 p>0.5。同模型采样的 reasoning traces 严重违反独立性假设（Fleiss' κ = 0.21–0.82，Eff-K 仅 1.20–3.63），解释了为什么简单增加 K 或添加符号约束不能突破 SC 的天花板。\\\\n\\\\n实验数据完美支持 dual requirement（κ 使用统一脚本 compute_fleiss_kappa_unified.py，SC/Δpp 使用 registry 原始值）：\\\\n- **Qwen family**: κ=0.66-0.82, Eff-K≈1.20-1.46（独立性不足）→ all near-zero delta，无论 SC 多高\\\\n- **Mistral-7B FOLIO**: κ≈0.46, Eff-K≈2.0（中等独立性）+ SC≈55%（中等质量）→ +3.43pp best seed, mean +2.12pp (2/3 seeds positive, 1 null)\\\\n- **Mistral-7B PW-600**: κ=0.20, Eff-K=3.76（最高独立性）但 SC=39%（质量不足）→ -2.83pp\\\\n- 只有 Goldilocks zone（中等 Eff-K + 中等 SC）出现正向 delta\\\\n- **Dual bottleneck 直接验证 (exp-055)**：Multi-temp κ=0.427 Eff-K=2.11（比单温度 κ≈0.46 Eff-K≈2.0 更独立）但 delta=0.00pp → 提高 independence 不够，还需要 extraction quality\\\\n- **T sensitivity 孤立尖峰 (4/4 complete, D064 corrected)**：T=0.3(Δ=-1.47), T=0.5(Δ=-0.98), T=0.7(Δ=+3.43, κ=0.460, Eff-K=1.98), T=1.0(Δ=0.00, κ=0.394, Eff-K=2.25)。T=0.7 孤立尖峰更突出。T=0.7 SC=55.88%（最低）→ entropy 最高 → SICA 操作空间最大。温度效应与 SC 效应互为 confound，论文需讨论\\\\n- **Thinking mode κ=0.913, Eff-K=1.09 (exp-028b)**：近乎确定性。思维模式让 traces 更一致 → 独立性更差。\\\\\\\"更好的推理\\\\\\\"反而加剧 independence bottleneck。\\\\n\\\\n**κ 是 model-level 稳定属性**：统一处理后 Mistral 三 seed κ 收敛至 0.457-0.460（之前 0.41-0.50 差异是非标准 answer 处理的 artifact）。κ 跨 seed 几乎相同，但 delta 差异大（+3.43 vs +2.94 vs 0.00）→ seed-level constraint quality variance 主导，非 κ。\\\\n\\\\n完整 κ 表（⚠️ D062: κ 将用 raw answers 重算，当前 unified 值为临时值。Qwen 无变化，Mistral 条件将更新）：\\\\n| Exp | Model | κ(unified→raw pending) |\\\\n\\\\n... majority voting 有帮助，但 SICA constraint extraction 无法利用这种 diversity → bottleneck 在 extraction 而非 trace diversity\\n   - 原 6 种：contrastive prompting, cross-model extraction, generational scaling, solver-guided refinement, consistency filtering, cross-trace UNSAT\\n   - 9 种修复涵盖 prompting / architecture / capability / verification / diversity / filtering 全维度，全部失败 → trap is fundamental\\n\\n## D070/D074 Update: Method-Level Interventions Also Fail — \\\"Fundamental Limitation\\\" Confirmed\\n\\n### Three Method Lines × Two Datasets = All Negative (D074)\\n\\nD070 从纯 analysis 升级为 analysis + method paper，但三条方法线全面失败（D074 确认），反而强化了原始 analysis 结论。\\n\\n**方法×数据集完整结果**：\\n| Method | FOLIO-204 | LogiQA-200 | Failure Mode |\\n|--------|-----------|------------|--------------|\\n| ICG (Independent Constraint Gen.) | Δ=-2.0pp (3-seed: -2.0/+1.5/-1.0, mean=-0.5pp±1.8pp, 噪声) | Δ=-0.5pp | Neutral — 独立约束质量≈trace-sourced约束 |\\n| Contrastive (Pro/Con Scoring) | Δ≈-16pp | Δ≈-7pp (interim) | Harmful — 26% degenerate scoring, pro/con 不平衡 |\\n| Debate (Proposer/Critic) | Δ=-18.6pp | Δ≈-28pp (interim) | Catastrophic — 7B 模型角色扮演严重退化推理 |\\n\\n**三种失败模式的分析价值**（论文 \\\"Why Methods Fail\\\" section）：\\n1. **ICG (neutral)**: 独立于 traces 生成的约束不比 trace-sourced 约束更好。说明 bottleneck 不仅是 \\\"约束来自 traces\\\"，而是模型的推理偏差从问题理解阶段就存在——独立约束和 trace-sourced 约束继承相同的 model bias。\\n2. **Contrastive (harmful)**: 强制 pro/con 评估引入 degenerate scoring（26% 问题无法有效区分）。7B 模型无法可靠生成平衡的对立约束——pro 约束系统性多于 con（94 pro vs 65 con），confirmation bias 从约束提取转移到了约束生成。\\n3. **Debate (catastrophic)**: Proposer/Critic/Defense 多角色链在 7B 模型上导致答案质量严重退化。辩论不仅未增加多样性，反而引入了角色扮演的额外认知负担。同模型辩论 = 同源性 + 角色扮演退化，双重损失。\\n\\n**Scale limitation caveat**: 所有方法测试在 7B 模型上。7B 模型生成 Z3 格式 FOL 约束的能力不足（ICG 多题 \\\"0/N constraints parsed\\\"）。14B+ 模型可能有不同结果——这既是 limitation 也是 future work 入口。\\n\\n### κ Generalization — Independence-Accuracy Trade-off (D075, CAPSTONE FINDING)\\n\\nexp-077 测量三种聚合方式在 Mistral-7B FOLIO-204 上的 κ 和 Eff-K：\\n| 聚合方式 | Fleiss' κ | Eff-K | 名义 K |\\n|----------|----------|-------|--------|\\n| Self-Consistency | 0.4384 | 2.02 | 10 |\\n| Diverse-Prompt | 0.4030 | 2.21 | 12 |\\n| LLM-Debate | 0.1549 | 4.44 | 12 |\\n\\n结合 exp-076 Debate Δ=-18.6pp 的准确率数据，揭示**同模型框架下的 fundamental independence-accuracy trade-off**：\\n- **SC/Diverse-Prompt**: κ~0.4，traces 高度相关但准确率正常。换 prompt 策略不改善独立性（Δκ=0.04）。\\n- **Debate**: κ=0.15，成功打破 trace 相关性（Eff-K 从 2.0 跃升到 4.4），但准确率崩溃 -18.6pp。低 κ 不代表\\\"更独立的推理\\\"——是 disagreement amplification（系统性噪声）。\\n- **Trade-off 本质**: 同一模型的推理能力是固定的。要么输出一致（高 κ, 正确但不独立），要么输出分歧（低 κ, 独立但不正确）。Condorcet Jury Theorem 要求 both independent AND accurate，同模型框架下无法同时满足。\\n\\n**论文叙事升级**: 从 \\\"methods fail\\\" → \\\"independence-accuracy trade-off is fundamental to same-model reasoning\\\"。突破瓶颈需要跨模型方案（不同模型天然更独立），Oracle 实验（gold FOL 来自外部知识源）已间接验证了这一点。\\n\\n### New Claim 6 (D070/D074)\\n\\n6. **Method-Level Interventions Cannot Break the Bottleneck**: 三种针对 independence bottleneck 不同环节的方法（ICG→循环依赖, Contrastive→confirmation bias, Debate→trace 同质性）在 2 个数据集上全部失败。加上之前 9 种 prompt-level 修复，共 12 种修复尝试全败。这证明 independence bottleneck 不是单一环节的问题，而是 same-model self-extraction 的根本性限制。\\n\\n### Updated Headline Result (D074)\\n\\n\\\"Across 6+ models and 4 domains, symbolic consensus aggregation shows no statistically significant positive effect. Three method-level interventions designed to break specific aspects of the independence bottleneck (independent constraint generation, contrastive scoring, debate-augmented extraction) all fail across two datasets, with two methods actively degrading performance by 16-28pp. Combined with 9 prompt-level remediation failures, we provide the most comprehensive evidence to date that the independence bottleneck is fundamental to same-model reasoning aggregation.\\\"\\n\\n### Updated Story Spine (D074)\\n\\n- **Problem**: [unchanged]\\n- **Theoretical lens**: [unchanged]\\n- **Systematic evaluation**: [unchanged]\\n- **Regime characterization**: [unchanged]\\n- **Remediation failures strengthen thesis**: 9 prompt-level + 3 method-level = 12 interventions across diversity/architecture/independence/verification dimensions, all fail → bottleneck is fundamental\\n- **NEW — Method failure analysis**: Three methods targeting different bottleneck aspects show three distinct failure modes (neutral/harmful/catastrophic), revealing that the bottleneck operates at multiple levels simultaneously\\n- **Headline**: Independence bottleneck is structural to same-model self-extraction, not addressable by prompt engineering, architectural modifications, or targeted method-level interventions at the 7B scale\\n\\n## Core Insight (D060 Updated — Dual Bottleneck)\\n\\n**Dual Requirement Framework**: SICA 效应需要同时满足两个条件，缺一不可：\\n1. **Sufficient Independence** (Eff-K > threshold): traces 需要提供足够多的独立视角\\n2. **Sufficient Quality** (SC > ~50%): traces 需要有足够的正确率，否则约束是噪声\\n\\nCondorcet Jury Theorem 要求投票者独立且 p>0.5。同模型采样的 reasoning traces 严重违反独立性假设（Fleiss' κ = 0.21–0.82，Eff-K 仅 1.20–3.63），解释了为什么简单增加 K 或添加符号约束不能突破 SC 的天花板。\\n\\n实验数据完美支持 dual
